After time's up it stays red and counts the overrun as +m:ss.\n• Tap anywhere to start. The controls fade while the clock runs and the screen stays awake.\n• A touch lock ignores stray taps on the lectern.\n• +1 and −1 move the end by a minute, and the color cues move with it. Touch and hold for 10 seconds, 30 seconds or 5 minutes.\n• A progress bar with a mark at each cue, the time of day and a flash at zero, each of them optional.\n• A chime or bell and a vibration at each cue. Sound follows the silent switch unless you tell it not to.\n• A color-blind palette: blue, yellow and orange.\n\nTimers\n• Starts simple with three timers: a club speech, a lightning talk and a conference talk. Templates add evaluations, pitches, sermons and more in one tap.\n• Add as many of your own as you like, from 5 seconds to 24 hours, each with its own cues, look and sound.\n• Quick-start lengths from 1 to 20 minutes, any custom length, or a timer that ends at a clock time, such as 8:30.\n\nFor timekeepers\n• Qualifying time windows with a grace period, judged in whole seconds: each run is marked qualified, under time or over time.\n• History keeps every run with the speaker's name and the actual time.\n• Share the day's timer's report as plain text in one tap, ready to read out at a speech club or paste into the group chat.\n\nControl it from your seat\n• Put an iPad on the lectern as the display and control it from your iPhone: start, pause, add time and move to the next speaker.\n• The two devices connect to each other directly over Wi-Fi or peer-to-peer, with a four-digit code. A started run even survives the app being closed.\n• Your timers and history stay on your devices. A second device connects to this one directly, never through a server.\n\nMade for speech-club timekeepers, conference and event organizers, AV crews, pastors, teachers, debate coaches and anyone rehearsing a talk.\n\nTalklight is free: every feature, no ads, no account, no subscription. "description":"Kalshi is the largest federally regulated prediction market app in the U.S., where you can make money by predicting real-world events, including football, basketball, hockey, and other sports, plus crypto prices. Perfect for sports, crypto, and event trading, forecasting, futures-style markets, and real-world predictions.\nIt’s like trading stocks – but instead, you trade on events you understand. Predict whether an event will happen or not, and earn money if you’re right.\nJoin 10M+ users trading thousands of prediction markets across finance, politics, weather, culture, sports, and more.\n\nFINANCIAL MARKETS\nDaily S&P 500, Dow, Nasdaq 100, Bitcoin, Gold, WTI oil, Company earnings\n\nECONOMIC MARKETS\nFed interest rates, Inflation (CPI), Jobs reports, GDP, Recession, Gas prices, Mortgage rates\n\nCLIMATE MARKETS\nDaily city temperatures, Hurricane strength, Tornado numbers\n\nCULTURE MARKETS\nBillboard 100, Netflix Top 10, Oscars, Grammys, #1 Hits\n\nHOW KALSHI WORKS\nKalshi is the largest federally regulated prediction exchange where you buy and sell contracts based on event outcomes. Contracts cost between 1¢ and 99¢ and can be sold anytime. At market close, each correct contract is worth $1.\n\nTRADE SPORTS\nKalshi lets you trade on real sports outcomes across football, basketball, hockey, soccer, MMA, tennis, golf, and more. \nTrade pro and college sports with highly liquid prediction markets. Trade live as games unfold, or combine multiple outcomes into one trade with Combos.\nKalshi is a Preferred Prediction Market Exchange of the NBA, bringing official NBA data to basketball markets.\n\nHOW IS KALSHI REGULATED?\nKalshi is federally regulated as a Designated Contract Market (DCM) by the Commodity Futures Trading Commission (CFTC). Kalshi’s affiliate, Kalshi Klear LLC, is a CFTC-regulated clearinghouse that holds member funds and clears trades.\n\nTRADE PERPETUALS\nPerpetual futures, or perps, let you trade the price of an asset without owning it. Go long if you expect the price to rise, or short if you expect it to fall. Perps have no expiration date, so you choose when to close your position.\nOn Kalshi, you can trade perps on crypto (Bitcoin, Ethereum, Solana, XRP), metals (gold, silver, copper), oil, and U.S. stock indexes, in the same app as your prediction markets. Gold and silver perps trade 24/7.\n\nKALSHI VS. STOCKS\nEvent contracts are direct. You trade the outcome of an event, not the future stock price of a company. In stock trading, you can be right and still lose money due to market sentiment or news. Prediction markets remove that layer.\n\nKALSHI VS. OPTIONS\nEvent contracts are simpler. Options pricing depends on multiple complex factors. Kalshi contracts reflect straightforward event probabilities. No time decay. If the event odds stay the same, contract value stays stable.\n\nHOW MUCH MONEY DO I NEED TO START?\nOpen and maintain a Kalshi account for free. Prediction markets require less capital than traditional trading, making it easier to diversify without risking large amounts.\n\n18+ only. Restrictions and eligibility requirements apply. Trading event contracts and trading leveraged products, including perpetual futures, involves significant risk and is not appropriate for everyone. Please carefully consider if it is appropriate for you in light of your personal financial circumstances. Not representative of overall customer performance or success. Past results are not necessarily indicative of future performance. \nKalshi products are not available in all jurisdictions. See kalshi.com/regulatory for more information.\nAdditional perpetual contract offerings are subject to applicable regulatory review and approval, and availability may vary.\nLeverage amplifies both potential gains and potential losses, and losses can exceed the amount deposited.
